Reconstructed narrative context and identified resumption point

Reconstructed narrative context and identified resumption point

**SESSION SUMMARY:**

Protagonist was granted "The Looting Power" by the Grandmaster via Iso-8 integration. Over multiple months and 41+ encounters, he has built a fully-equipped armored motor-home/mobile fortress with progressive technology integration (Sentinel, Chitauri, Doombot, demon-stone, mystical wards, etc.). S.H.I.E.L.D. arrangement established weeks ago - supply boxes in exchange for handling threats. Expanded partnership now includes coordinated operations. Fantastic Four contacted protagonist for Negative Zone dimensional incursion coordination. Protagonist has just arrived at the Baxter Building with his motor-home. Sue Storm, Reed Richards, Ben Grimm are outside the vehicle examining it. Johnny Storm is watching from above. Sue has asked protagonist to indicate briefing preference (conference room or vehicle-based). Mission is time-sensitive - dimensional portal formation probability 87% within next 12 hours.

---

**KNOWLEDGE TRACKING:**

- **Protagonist:** Inside motor-home; just arrived at Baxter Building; aware of Fantastic Four coordination request; knows mission is dimensional incursion related; unaware of Iso-8, Grandmaster, or threat-attraction mechanism; has not yet responded to Sue's briefing location question
- **Sue Storm:** Outside vehicle; made briefing location offer; managing Reed and Ben; coordinating mission; has seen surveillance photos but this is first in-person view of full vehicle scale
- **Reed Richards:** Outside vehicle; fascinated by demon-stone integration and new upgrades; knows vehicle upgraded twice in past three days; struggling not to immediately request examination access; aware of 12-hour timeline
- **Ben Grimm:** Outside vehicle; tactically assessing capabilities; impressed by professional operational setup; noted defensive positioning and active force field
- **Johnny Storm:** Watching from window above; wants to examine vehicle; has been told to wait for formal briefing
- **Nick Fury / S.H.I.E.L.D.:** Aware of Fantastic Four coordination; monitoring
- **Grandmaster:** Observing this new collaboration variable with interest

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue maintains her position outside the motor-home.

Professional. Patient. She's made the offer - conference room or vehicle-based briefing - and now she's waiting for the response.

Behind her, Reed is still visibly restraining himself from approaching the demon-stone integration points. Ben has completed his tactical assessment but is staying alert. Johnny's silhouette is visible in the window above.

The motor-home sits there. Forty feet of armored presence. Active systems humming.

The twelve-hour window is ticking.

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed forces himself to stop analyzing the visible upgrades.

He pulls up the dimensional data on his tablet instead - refocusing on the actual mission priority.

"Portal formation probability has increased to ninety-one percent in the last forty minutes," he says aloud, partly to himself, partly to the others. "We're running out of preparation time."

He glances at the motor-home.

Still no response from inside.

---

**BEN GRIMM**

Ben shifts his weight slightly.

"Kid's probably runnin' his own tactical assessment," he observes. "New location, new team, time-sensitive op. Smart play is to evaluate before committing."

He's not impatient. Just noting the operational logic.

---

The Baxter Building entrance area is quiet except for the hum of the motor-home's active systems and the ambient city noise.

Waiting for the protagonist's choice.

Salvage opens the door. "Come on inside."

Verified rule compliance and prepared character response

Verified rule compliance and prepared character response

**SESSION SUMMARY:**

Protagonist was granted "The Looting Power" by the Grandmaster via Iso-8 integration. 41+ encounters. S.H.I.E.L.D. arrangement established with expanded coordination. Fantastic Four contacted for Negative Zone dimensional incursion (portal formation 91% probability within 12 hours). Motor-home arrived at Baxter Building. Sue, Reed, Ben, and Johnny assembled at vehicle entrance. Protagonist opened door, invited them inside, and acknowledged the vehicle's impressive nature with no offense taken at their reactions.

---

**KNOWLEDGE TRACKING:**

- **Protagonist:** Door open; has invited FF inside twice; acknowledged their impressed reactions; waiting inside
- **Sue Storm:** Invitation accepted; entering vehicle with team
- **Reed Richards:** About to enter; already observing interior systems
- **Ben Grimm:** Entering; recognized trust gesture
- **Johnny Storm:** Just arrived; impressed; trying to be professional
- **S.H.I.E.L.D.:** Monitoring
- **Grandmaster:** Observing FF integration

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

Johnny grins briefly. "Right? I mean, the tactical applications alone—"

Sue cuts him off with a look.

"Mission briefing, Johnny."

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue enters first.

The interior is significantly larger than the exterior suggested - spatial optimization through the integrated systems. She takes in the layout quickly: command/operations area, equipment storage, living quarters section, workstation setups. Everything is organized for function, not aesthetics. Combat-ready. Mobile and self-sufficient.

The demon-stone integration is visible throughout - dark veining through structural supports, faint luminescence in the power distribution network.

She moves far enough inside to make room for the others.

"Impressive operational setup," she says. Direct, professional assessment.

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed enters second, tablet still in hand.

His eyes track across the visible systems immediately. Energy distribution network. Mystical ward integration points. The technological-magical hybrid architecture that shouldn't work according to conventional physics but clearly does.

"The spatial interior doesn't match exterior dimensions," he observes aloud. Technical fascination breaking through. "Dimensional folding? Mystical expansion? Both?"

He catches himself. Refocuses.

"Sorry. Mission first. Scientific curiosity second." He means it. Mostly.

---

**BEN GRIMM**

Ben enters third. He has to angle slightly to fit through the entrance - his mass requires it.

Once inside, he does a quick tactical sweep. Exits. Weapon placements. Power systems. Defensive coverage.

"Solid work," he says simply. Not elaborate praise. Just professional recognition from someone who knows mobile combat platforms.

He positions himself where he can see both the entrance and the interior - tactical habit.

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

Johnny enters last, looking around with open curiosity.

"This started as a motorcycle?" He's trying to imagine the progression. "How many upgrades did it take to get to—" He gestures at the full interior. "—this?"

Sue gives him another look.

"Right. Mission. Focusing." He closes his mouth but continues looking around.

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue turns her attention to the task at hand.

"We have approximately twelve hours before portal formation becomes critical. Negative Zone incursion point has been triangulated to industrial sector, approximately eight miles from here." She pulls up a map on her own tablet. "Reed has dimensional containment protocols ready. We need to coordinate combat response, containment deployment, and civilian evacuation if necessary."

She looks at the interior setup.

"You have tactical display capabilities here? We can run the mission brief on your systems or I can project from my tablet."

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed is already pulling up the dimensional scan data.

"The instability is producing energy signatures consistent with Negative Zone fauna - likely Annihilus's peripheral forces rather than organized invasion. But the portal size projections suggest something larger than standard reconnaissance."

He's trying to stay on task.

The demon-stone power integration three feet to his left is glowing faintly and he desperately wants to scan it.

Mission first.

---

The four members of the Fantastic Four are now inside the motor-home. Professional. Mission-focused. Ready to coordinate.

But also clearly impressed by the operational capability of the mobile fortress they're standing in.

The twelve-hour window continues counting down.

"You can use my stuff. And what, exactly, is the threat? Because if it isn't an embodied threat then I don't know if my ability can do anything."

Orchestrated character clarification and mission parameters alignment

Orchestrated character clarification and mission parameters alignment

**SESSION SUMMARY:**

Protagonist was granted "The Looting Power" by the Grandmaster via Iso-8 integration. 41+ encounters. S.H.I.E.L.D. arrangement established with expanded coordination. Fantastic Four contacted for Negative Zone dimensional incursion (portal formation 91% probability within 12 hours). All four FF members now inside motor-home for mission briefing. Protagonist has offered use of his systems and asked for threat clarification, explicitly noting his ability requires embodied threats.

---

**KNOWLEDGE TRACKING:**

- **Protagonist:** Offered use of vehicle systems; asked for threat specifics; has indicated his ability has limitation (requires embodied threats); waiting for explanation
- **Sue Storm:** Just learned protagonist's ability has specific trigger requirements; about to coordinate briefing
- **Reed Richards:** Preparing to explain threat nature; now knows protagonist's capability has constraints
- **Ben Grimm:** Listening; learning operational parameters
- **Johnny Storm:** Learning about protagonist's ability for first time
- **S.H.I.E.L.D.:** Monitoring
- **Grandmaster:** Observing

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed looks up from his tablet.

"Embodied threats. Physical entities." He's processing the limitation against the mission parameters. "Yes. The Negative Zone incursion will manifest as fauna - creatures, organisms. Potentially Annihilus's scout forces or territorial species displaced by dimensional instability."

He pulls up imagery on his tablet - biological scans, previous encounter documentation.

"These aren't energy phenomena or abstract dimensional forces. They're living organisms with physical forms. Hostile, aggressive, and very much tangible." He pauses. "Your ability would apply."

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue nods, connecting the information.

"Good. Then we have combat coverage." She's running tactical calculation. "Reed handles containment and portal closure. Ben and I provide frontline engagement. Johnny covers aerial threats and high-mobility response."

She looks at Salvage.

"You'd be additional combat capacity against whatever comes through. S.H.I.E.L.D. flagged you as effective against enhanced threats - this qualifies."

---

**BEN GRIMM**

Ben speaks up.

"How many we expectin'?" He's asking Reed. "Scout group? Full incursion? Need to know what we're preppin' for."

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed adjusts data on his tablet.

"Portal diameter projections suggest anywhere from six to twenty individual entities initially. Potentially more if the breach stabilizes." He's reading the dimensional flux measurements. "Size variance is high - anywhere from human-scale to fifteen feet tall based on previous Negative Zone fauna documentation."

He looks at Salvage.

"If you need physical contact with defeated entities, combat positioning will matter. We can coordinate engagement zones to ensure you have access."

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

Johnny is following along, piecing together what he's learning.

"So you fight things and then… what, exactly?" He's asking directly, genuinely curious about the mechanic.

Sue gives him a warning look - this might be sensitive operational information.

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue intercedes smoothly.

"Salvage, you don't have to explain your capabilities in detail if you'd prefer not to. We can coordinate tactically without full disclosure." She's being professional. Respecting operational security. "What we need to know: effective combat range, preferred engagement distance, any support requirements."

She's giving him the choice of how much to share.

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed is already thinking through tactical positioning.

"If we establish containment perimeter here—" He gestures at his tablet display. "—and coordinate engagement zones, we can maximize both combat efficiency and whatever your specific operational requirements are."

He's being respectful of the information boundary Sue just set while still trying to plan effectively.

---

The motor-home interior is now mission command center. Four members of the Fantastic Four coordinating with an enhanced independent operative for time-sensitive dimensional threat.

Portal formation probability: 91% within twelve hours.

Threat type: Confirmed physical entities. Protagonist's ability applicable.

Tactical coordination in progress.

"Simple, defeat a bad guy, touch, all the mass becomes equipment. Somehow. No idea how but it has been consistent for weeks. And my base has weapon systems so that'll be nice. If we defeat a single dimensional beast and I make contact, then I can integrate it on the spot. Meaning we get increasingly provisioned every time I make contact. Fortunately, it works through gloves."

Protagonist was granted "The Looting Power" by the Grandmaster via Iso-8 integration. 41+ encounters. S.H.I.E.L.D. arrangement established with expanded coordination. Fantastic Four inside motor-home for Negative Zone dimensional incursion briefing (portal formation 91% probability within 12 hours). Reed confirmed threat will be physical entities (Negative Zone fauna). Protagonist has now explained his ability mechanics to the FF: defeat hostile entity, physical contact, mass converts to equipment, mechanism unknown, works through gloves, integration happens on-site creating compounding provisioning.

---

- **Protagonist:** Explained ability to FF; offered vehicle weapon systems for mission; waiting for tactical coordination
- **Sue Storm:** Now understands basic mechanic and tactical implication (combat effectiveness increases during engagement); coordinating mission
- **Reed Richards:** Just learned mass-conversion mechanism with unknown underlying process; wants to study this; forcing mission focus
- **Ben Grimm:** Understands practical application for sustained combat

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed stops. Stares at Salvage.

"Mass conversion. Direct material transformation with perfect efficiency and you don't know the mechanism." He's processing. "That violates conservation of energy unless there's an external power source or dimensional…"

He catches himself. Visibly forces pause.

"Sorry. Fascinating. Extremely fascinating. But—" He looks at his tablet. "—mission first. Scientific inquiry later."

The effort this is taking him is visible.

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue is already recalculating tactical deployment.

"Compounding provisioning during active engagement." She's thinking through the implications. "That means extended combat favors us. Each defeated entity makes the next one easier to handle."

Professional. Mission-focused. Taking the operational advantage and integrating it into planning.

**BEN GRIMM**

"Snowball effect. More we fight, better equipped we get." He's appreciating the tactical simplicity. "Good for sustained operations. Means we don't gotta worry about runnin' outta firepower if this goes long."

Reed is pulling up tactical maps on his tablet with visible effort not to ask follow-up questions about the mass conversion.

"Your vehicle has mobility capability. Can you reposition during active engagement or does the weapon system require stationary deployment?"

Sue is coordinating the tactical overlay.

"We need fire lanes, fallback positions, and containment zones." She's thinking through the three-dimensional combat space. "Portal will manifest at ground level. Negative Zone fauna typically disperses radially from breach point."

She looks around the motor-home interior.

"Can you project tactical display here or should we use my systems?"

Orchestrated Fantastic Four reactions to advanced vehicle specifications

**SESSION SUMMARY:**

Protagonist was granted "The Looting Power" by the Grandmaster via Iso-8 integration. 41+ encounters. S.H.I.E.L.D. arrangement established with expanded coordination. Fantastic Four inside motor-home for Negative Zone dimensional incursion briefing (portal formation 91% probability within 12 hours). Protagonist explained ability mechanics and has now provided detailed vehicle specifications including: 40-foot armored mobile fortress with Sentinel/Doombot/Chitauri/Ultron/demon-stone integration, multiple weapon systems, force field, mystical wards (Doctor Strange-detectable at three blocks), advanced sensors (2-mile active/8-mile passive), dual AI cores, self-repair nanites, demon-forged upgrades, and multiple power sources including micro-fusion and dimensional substrate reactors.

---

**KNOWLEDGE TRACKING:**

- **Protagonist:** Provided full technical specifications to FF; waiting for tactical coordination
- **Sue Storm:** Reading specifications; coordinating tactical deployment
- **Reed Richards:** Reading specifications; struggling not to derail into scientific analysis
- **Ben Grimm:** Assessing firepower and defensive capabilities
- **Johnny Storm:** Impressed by specifications
- **S.H.I.E.L.D.:** Monitoring
- **Grandmaster:** Observing

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed reads the specifications.

His expression goes through several distinct phases in rapid succession.

"Dual AI cores. Self-repair nanites. Dimensional substrate power draw. Demon-stone integration with mystical ward output at Sanctum-grade levels." He's reading faster. "Multi-spectrum sensors including mystical wavelength detection. Atmospheric water generation. Micro-fusion reactor AND alien energy core AND dimensional—"

He stops himself with visible effort.

"This is…" He looks up at Salvage. "…tactically comprehensive."

The understatement is deliberate. He's forcing himself not to launch into detailed technical questions.

Sue notices.

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue is reading the tactical implications.

"Thirty-foot force field radius. Energy cannon with swivel mount. Defensive turrets. Eight-mile passive detection." She's building the operational picture. "You can establish a hardened firebase position with active threat detection and sustained fire support."

She looks at Reed.

"Portal manifestation point - eight miles falls within his passive sensor range. We can use the vehicle as early warning and fallback position."

Professional. Tactical. Using the capabilities for mission planning.

---

**BEN GRIMM**

Ben reads through the weapons systems section.

"Energy cannon, defensive turrets, mystical wards, force field generator." He looks around the interior with new appreciation. "This ain't a vehicle. This is a mobile firebase with living quarters."

He's impressed despite himself.

"Demon-forged energy arrays - those from the dimensional incursion two weeks ago?" He's asking Salvage directly. "S.H.I.E.L.D. report mentioned Omega-level event."

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

Johnny is staring at the specifications.

"Wait. Holographic disguise makes it look like a normal RV?" He glances around the interior. "So this whole thing just… drives around looking like someone's vacation vehicle?"

He grins.

"That's actually hilarious."

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed is still processing the technical specifications.

"The self-repair nanites - integrated from which encounter?" He can't quite help himself. "And the dual AI architecture - are they collaborative or hierarchical? The mystical ward strength suggests either massive power throughput or extremely efficient—"

Sue clears her throat.

Reed stops.

"Right. Mission." He refocuses on the tactical display. "Using the vehicle as sensor platform and firebase. Positioning here—" He marks the map. "—gives coverage of portal manifestation zone while maintaining fallback distance."

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue is coordinating the tactical overlay.

"Salvage, can the AI cores provide real-time threat analysis during engagement? If your sensors can identify and categorize Negative Zone fauna, that gives us significant tactical advantage."

She's looking at the communication systems specifications.

"Encrypted comms across multiple bands - we can integrate you into our tactical network. Reed, can we patch Salvage's systems into our coordination frequency?"

---

**REED RICHARDS**

"Yes. Standard protocol. I'll need access to the communication systems for frequency synchronization but that's—" He glances at Salvage. "—if you're comfortable with that level of integration."

He's being respectful of operational security while trying to maximize mission effectiveness.

---

**BEN GRIMM**

Ben is thinking through combat deployment.

"Vehicle positions as hardpoint. Reed works containment. Sue and I handle frontline. Johnny covers aerial." He looks at Salvage. "You workin' from the vehicle or deploying on foot?"

It's a practical question. Fire support versus direct engagement. Both have tactical value.

---

Orchestrated Fantastic Four's tactical response to protagonist's formidable integrated loadout

**SESSION SUMMARY:**

Protagonist was granted "The Looting Power" by the Grandmaster via Iso-8 integration. 41+ encounters. S.H.I.E.L.D. arrangement established with expanded coordination. Fantastic Four inside motor-home for Negative Zone dimensional incursion briefing (portal formation 91% within 12 hours). Protagonist provided vehicle specifications and has now provided personal loadout specifications including: AIM computational exosuit base, Sentinel/Kree/Hand/Wrecking Crew/Inhuman/LMD/Ultron integrations, neural interface, exo-frame strength enhancement (triple capacity), multiple weapon systems, stealth field, mystical wards, advanced sensors, hard-light shield, and various tactical systems.

---

**KNOWLEDGE TRACKING:**

- **Protagonist:** Provided full personal loadout specs to FF; waiting for tactical coordination
- **Sue Storm:** Reading personal capabilities; coordinating tactical deployment based on actual combat capacity
- **Reed Richards:** Reading specifications; desperately trying to stay mission-focused
- **Ben Grimm:** Assessing combat effectiveness and integration complexity
- **Johnny Storm:** Impressed by capability array
- **S.H.I.E.L.D.:** Monitoring
- **Grandmaster:** Observing

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed reads the personal loadout specifications.

His hand tightens slightly on his tablet.

"Neural interface with thirty percent reaction time enhancement. Kree exo-frame. Sentinel threat recognition integrated with LMD combat prediction. Ultron-derived AI threat assessment. Mystical ward talisman. Hardlight construction capability. Harmonic resonance weaponry—"

He stops himself. Takes a breath.

"You've integrated technology from at least twelve distinct hostile sources into a cohesive functional system with no apparent compatibility issues or rejection cascade."

He looks at Salvage directly.

He stops himself again.

Sue is watching him struggle. She appreciates the effort he's making.

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue pulls up the tactical map again.

"Revised deployment assessment." She's integrating the new information. "Salvage, with your capabilities you can operate in three roles: vehicle fire support, independent forward operations, or coordinated front-line engagement with Ben and myself."

She looks at him.

"What's your preference? The mission is flexible enough to accommodate any of those approaches."

---

Ben adds a practical consideration.

"If you're deployiIn' on foot, that exo-frame strength means you can handle Negative Zone fauna in direct engagement. Some of 'em hit hard - Kree enhancement should let you take the hits and return 'em."

He's thinking through combat matchups.

"The hard-light shield—four-foot barrier—that covers center mass defense. Good for tankin' hits while returnin' fire."

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

Johnny has a question.

"The stealth field and sonic dampening - can you use those for ambush tactics? If these things come through the portal in a group, you could potentially pick them off from concealment before they organize."

He's actually thinking tactically.

Sue looks mildly surprised.

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed forces himself back to the mission timeline.

"Portal manifestation in approximately eleven hours, forty minutes." He pulls up the latest dimensional readings. "Energy flux is increasing. We should begin pre-positioning within the next six to eight hours to ensure containment readiness."

He looks at the specifications one more time.

The effort not to ask about the integration process is visible.

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue is coordinating the final tactical framework.

"We have vehicle capabilities, personal combat loadout, and team composition mapped. Next step: specific positioning, engagement rules, and fallback protocols."

She looks around the motor-home interior.

"Can we use your tactical display systems or should we coordinate on my equipment?"

---

The Fantastic Four now have complete technical understanding of both vehicle and personal capabilities. Reed is barely maintaining focus on the mission instead of the scientific implications. Sue is professionally coordinating. Ben is assessing combat synergy. Johnny is impressed and actually thinking tactically.

Portal formation timeline: Approximately 11 hours, 40 minutes.

The protagonist has provided unprecedented operational transparency for a first coordination mission.

The specifications reveal months of progressive combat integration from dozens of hostile sources.

Mission planning ready to move into specific tactical deployment.

Night. The industrial sector is abandoned - warehouses and processing facilities shut down years ago. The area was cleared by S.H.I.E.L.D. six hours ago under cover of "gas leak investigation." No civilians within half-mile radius.

The motor-home is positioned exactly one-eighth mile from the coordinates Reed calculated. Force field inactive to avoid interfering with dimensional readings. Weapon systems on standby. Dual AI cores are active, processing sensor data.

The air splits.

Front-line combat is now active.

Orchestrated progressive combat montage with equipment layering and vehicle manifestation mechanics

**SESSION SUMMARY:**

Protagonist was granted "The Looting Power" by the Grandmaster via Iso-8 integration. 41+ encounters. Fantastic Four coordination mission for Negative Zone dimensional incursion now active. Portal manifested, 12 entities emerged. Combat engagement proceeding over ~26 minutes until portal closure. Salvage defeated serpentine entity, made contact, generated dimensionally hardened serpent equipment. Used hard-light path to reach Ben's defeated insectoid. Ben received dimensionally hardened insectoid equipment with zero adjustment needed. Pattern established: Sue, Reed, and Johnny defeat entities, Salvage makes contact, they each receive equipment. Layered equipment builds progressively, increasing effectiveness. Reached tipping point: generation shifted to dimensionally hardened biotech vehicles that grow over time. Salvage's vehicle receiving dimensional firming upgrades via streams of energy. Salvage did NOT receive a new vehicle himself.

---

**KNOWLEDGE TRACKING:**

- **Protagonist:** Combat ongoing; making contact with all defeated entities; vehicle receiving dimensional firming upgrades; coordinating with FF team
- **Sue Storm:** Receiving equipment from defeated entities via Salvage contact; experiencing progressive capability increase; observing vehicle manifestation phenomenon
- **Reed Richards:** Receiving equipment from defeated entities; monitoring containment while experiencing unprecedented equipment generation
- **Ben Grimm:** Received first equipment (insectoid-derived), noticed zero adjustment needed and immediate capability enhancement; continuing front-line combat with progressive upgrades
- **Johnny Storm:** Receiving equipment from aerial kills; experiencing progressive buildup
- **S.H.I.E.L.D.:** Monitoring
- **Grandmaster:** Observing team distribution mechanic with extreme interest

---

**BEN GRIMM - FIRST EQUIPMENT INTEGRATION**

The dimensionally hardened insectoid equipment materializes on Ben.

Chitinous plating overlays his rocky hide - not replacing, _integrating_. The dimensional hardening weaves through his natural armor like it was always meant to be there.

Ben flexes his hands. Rolls his shoulders.

"Huh." He sounds surprised. "Don't even feel it. Just feel… tougher."

The third insectoid charges him.

Ben meets it head-on. His punch impacts with noticeably more force. The creature's carapace shatters on first strike instead of second.

"Okay, yeah. This is workin'." He's already moving to the next target. "Whatever you're doin', Salvage - keep doin' it."

---

**SUE STORM - FIRST EQUIPMENT INTEGRATION**

Sue defeats a quadrupedal armored creature with concentrated force field crush.

Salvage makes contact.

Equipment manifests on Sue - dimensional armor that doesn't interfere with her own suit, integrated so smoothly it looks like it was part of the original design. Protective plating at vital points. Energy distribution network that enhances her force field projection.

She generates a barrier immediately - testing.

The field is _stronger_. Noticeably. Same effort, better output.

"Reed, are you seeing this?" She's maintaining combat coordination while processing the phenomenon. "The equipment is enhancing my powers, not just protecting me."

---

**REED RICHARDS - FIRST EQUIPMENT INTEGRATION**

Reed neutralizes a floating jellyfish entity with a dimensional disruption pulse from his equipment.

Salvage reaches him, makes contact.

Equipment generates across Reed's suit - dimensional sensors, enhanced analytical tools, processing upgrades that integrate with his existing tech.

Reed's eyes go wide behind his visor.

"Oh. Oh this is…" He's reading data flow that wasn't there three seconds ago. "I can _see_ the dimensional substrate now. The equipment is giving me sensory input I didn't have before."

He forces focus back to containment monitoring.

But he's cataloguing everything. Every enhancement. Every integration point.

---

**JOHNNY STORM - FIRST EQUIPMENT INTEGRATION**

Johnny incinerates the winged creature mid-flight.

It drops. Salvage is already moving to it.

Contact made.

Equipment manifests on Johnny - dimensional wing structures that don't interfere with his flame, thermal regulation systems, aerial mobility enhancements.

Johnny immediately tests them.

"Whoa!" He's flying faster. Sharper turns. Better control. "This is like upgrading from a prop plane to a jet!"

He spots another aerial target emerging from the final portal wave.

Intercepts it twice as fast as he could have before.

---

**PROGRESSIVE BUILDUP - FIVE MINUTES IN**

Six entities defeated. Six equipment integrations distributed across the team.

Ben is moving faster despite his mass. Hitting harder. Taking hits that would have staggered him and shrugging them off.

Sue's force fields are 40% stronger and she's generating them with less concentration.

Reed is processing dimensional data in real-time, adjusting containment parameters with enhanced precision.

Johnny is dominating the aerial space with improved mobility and thermal output.

Each subsequent entity falls faster than the last.

---

**PROGRESSIVE BUILDUP - TEN MINUTES IN**

Nine entities defeated. The equipment is layering.

Ben has chitinous plating, dimensional armor, enhanced strength integration, impact absorption systems. All of it fits perfectly. All of it just makes him _more_.

"It's stackin'!" He calls out over comm. "Every piece makes the next one work better!"

He's right. The equipment isn't redundant - it's synergistic.

Sue has defensive plating, force field amplifiers, energy channeling networks, protective barriers that activate automatically. Her tactical coordination is enhanced by processing support she didn't have before.

Reed has sensor suites, analytical tools, dimensional perception modules, calculation accelerators. He's tracking variables he couldn't even detect previously.

Johnny has aerial enhancement, thermal regulation, wing structures, speed boosters. He's a completely different class of aerial combatant than he was fifteen minutes ago.

---

**THE TIPPING POINT - FIFTEEN MINUTES IN**

Eleventh entity defeated.

Salvage makes contact.

Something changes.

Instead of equipment manifesting on Ben, a _vehicle_ begins to form.

Dimensionally hardened biotech. Insectoid-derived. It starts small - motorcycle-sized - but it's _growing_. Incorporating dimensional energy from the surrounding area. Building itself.

Ben stares at it.

"Uh. Salvage? That supposed to happen?"

---

**VEHICLE MANIFESTATION - SUE**

Twelfth entity defeated by Sue.

Contact made.

A vehicle manifests near her position. Biotech. Serpentine design. Dimensionally hardened. Growing.

Sue watches it take shape.

"Reed, are you recording this?" She's maintaining tactical awareness while observing unprecedented phenomenon. "The generation pattern just changed classification entirely."

---

**VEHICLE MANIFESTATION - REED**

Reed defeats an entity attempting to breach the containment perimeter.

Salvage makes contact.

Reed's vehicle manifests. Biotech. Heavily sensor-equipped. Analytical systems visible in the organic-technological hybrid structure. Growing steadily.

Reed is _transfixed_.

"It's not just generating equipment anymore. It's generating entire _systems_." He's scanning his own vehicle with handheld equipment. "Autonomous biotech vehicles with dimensional hardening and… oh this is extraordinary…"

Sue's voice cuts through: "Reed. Containment status?"

Reed snaps back to task.

"Twenty minutes to portal closure. Containment stable. I'm focused." He reluctantly pulls his attention away from the vehicle.

---

**VEHICLE MANIFESTATION - JOHNNY**

Johnny defeats the last aerial threat.

Contact made.

His vehicle manifests. Winged biotech. Built for flight. Dimensionally hardened. Growing.

Johnny circles it while airborne.

"Okay this is officially the coolest thing that's ever happened on a mission." He's genuinely excited. "I have a living flying vehicle now. That's MINE."

---

**SALVAGE'S VEHICLE - DIMENSIONAL FIRMING STREAMS**

While the FF members' vehicles are manifesting and growing, Salvage's motor-home is receiving something different.

Streams of dimensional energy flow from each defeated entity back to the vehicle. Not building a new vehicle - enhancing the existing one.

The dimensional hardening intensifies. The armor plating gains additional layers of dimensional firmness. The vehicle's ability to resist dimensional stress increases exponentially.

The AI cores register the upgrades in real-time:

_"Dimensional resistance increasing. Current threshold: moderate dimensional collapse survivable. Dimensional navigation protocols… developing."_

_"Vehicle mass increasing through dimensional integration. Structural integrity enhanced. Power draw from dimensional substrate optimized."_

---

**BEN GRIMM - OBSERVING HIS VEHICLE**

Ben's biotech vehicle has grown to substantial size. Insectoid-derived. Heavily armored. Built for his mass and combat style.

He walks around it slowly.

"It's… alive?" He's not sure how to process this. "And it's mine?"

The vehicle responds to his proximity. Shifts slightly. Recognition.

"Huh." Ben touches the chitinous plating. "Guess I got a ride now."

---

**SUE STORM - TACTICAL OBSERVATION**

Sue is watching all four vehicles manifest and grow while maintaining combat awareness.

"The power is distributing rewards based on contribution and role." She's analyzing the pattern. "Each vehicle matches the operator - Ben's is armored and heavy, mine has force field integration, Reed's is sensor-equipped, Johnny's flies."

She looks toward Salvage's motor-home.

"And Salvage's existing vehicle is being upgraded instead of replaced."

Professional analysis. Filing everything away.

---

**REED RICHARDS - SCIENTIFIC RESTRAINT FAILING**

Reed is trying to monitor containment.

He's also watching four biotech vehicles grow from defeated dimensional entities.

The containment can mostly run itself at this point.

"The generation intelligence is remarkable," he says into the comm. "It assessed each team member's capabilities, combat role, and current equipment status, then generated appropriate vehicles that enhance rather than duplicate existing advantages."

He's scanning his own vehicle.

"Mine has dimensional sensor arrays that integrate with my analytical framework. The biotechnology is responsive to neural input - I can already detect the interface protocols forming…"

Sue: "Reed. Portal closure time?"

Reed: "…Eighteen minutes. Sorry. Focused now."

He's not very convincing.

---

**JOHNNY STORM - TESTING HIS VEHICLE**

Johnny lands next to his winged biotech vehicle.

It responds to his presence - wings flex, bioluminescence pulses.

"Can I fly it NOW or do I have to wait until the mission's over?" He's asking Sue.

Sue: "Mission first, Johnny."

Johnny: "But it's RIGHT THERE and it has WINGS and—"

Sue: "Mission. First."

Johnny sighs but stays focused on aerial coverage.

The vehicle continues growing behind him.

---

**PORTAL CLOSURE - FINAL MINUTES**

Reed's containment field has compressed the portal to half its original diameter.

No more entities emerging. All twelve defeated and contacted.

The four FF vehicles are fully manifested - each one perfectly suited to its operator, dimensionally hardened, biotech-integrated, and clearly bonded to the specific team member.

Salvage's motor-home has absorbed massive dimensional firming. The vehicle is visibly more substantial. The dimensional resistance is now extreme-grade.

The AI cores confirm: _"Dimensional navigation protocols: 47% complete. Estimated full development: two to four additional dimensional encounters."_

---

**BEN GRIMM - POST-COMBAT ASSESSMENT**

Ben looks at his insectoid biotech vehicle. Looks at the layered equipment he's now wearing. Looks at Salvage.

"So. Every time we fight together, we get stronger." He's processing the tactical implication. "And now we got vehicles too."

He's impressed despite himself.

"S.H.I.E.L.D.'s gonna want reports on this."

---

**SUE STORM - MISSION COMPLETION**

Sue is confirming threat neutralization.

"All twelve entities defeated. No breaches. Containment holding." She looks at Reed. "Portal collapse status?"

Reed: "Fourteen minutes to full closure. Mission success."

Sue nods. Then looks at the four new vehicles and the extensively upgraded motor-home.

"Mission success," she confirms. "With… significant developments."

---

**REED RICHARDS - BARELY CONTAINED EXCITEMENT**

Reed is trying very hard to maintain professional demeanor.

He is failing.

"I need to examine these vehicles. Immediately. The biotechnology integration with dimensional hardening should be impossible but it's clearly functional and the neural interface protocols are developing in real-time and—"

Sue: "After portal closure, Reed."

Reed: "Yes. Right. After portal closure."

He looks at his vehicle again.

"But immediately after."

---

**GRANDMASTER - OBSERVATION**

En Dwi Gast is watching the data streams with extreme interest.

"Team distribution mechanic confirmed. The power adapts to group composition." He's analyzing the vehicle manifestations. "And it just escalated from equipment to autonomous vehicles."

He reviews Salvage's motor-home upgrades.

"Dimensional navigation protocols developing. At current progression rate, he'll have dimensional travel capability within three more encounters."

The Grandmaster makes a note.

"The game just expanded to multidimensional scope. New phase: confirmed."

---

The mission is nearing completion. Portal collapsing under Reed's containment. All threats neutralized. Each Fantastic Four member now possesses a dimensionally hardened biotech vehicle perfectly suited to their capabilities. Salvage's vehicle massively upgraded with extreme dimensional firming and developing dimensional navigation capability.

Reed is barely restraining scientific excitement. Ben is processing the force multiplication. Sue is professionally coordinating while analyzing the unprecedented phenomenon. Johnny wants to test-fly his new vehicle immediately.

Portal closure: Fourteen minutes remaining.

First collaborative mission with the Fantastic Four: overwhelming success with dramatic capability increases for all participants.

Salvage looks at all of them. “So. Your thoughts on our team up?”

Progressive buildup reached tipping point: dimensionally hardened biotech vehicles manifested for Ben, Sue, Reed, and Johnny - each perfectly suited to operator. Salvage has just asked the FF for their assessment of the teamup.

---

**KNOWLEDGE TRACKING:**

- **Protagonist:** Asked for FF's assessment; waiting for responses
- **Sue Storm:** Processing unprecedented equipment generation and team force multiplication; ready to provide assessment
- **Reed Richards:** Barely containing scientific excitement; trying to remain professional
- **Ben Grimm:** Has new equipment and biotech vehicle; impressed by practical effectiveness
- **Johnny Storm:** Enthusiastic about new vehicle and mission success

**BEN GRIMM**

Ben looks at Salvage directly.

"Effective." He's matter-of-fact about it. "We came in with what we had. We're leavin' with a hell of a lot more. Every fight made the next one easier. That's smart tactics."

He glances at his biotech vehicle - still growing slightly, stabilizing at full size.

"And I got a vehicle now. Which is weird. But useful." He looks back at Salvage. "You coordinate well, you handle yourself in close quarters, and you made the whole team stronger just by bein' here."

He pauses.

"S.H.I.E.L.D. was right to set this up. I'd work with you again."

Simple. Direct. High praise from Ben Grimm.

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

Johnny is still eyeing his winged vehicle but turns his attention to Salvage.

"Are you kidding? That was AWESOME." He's grinning. "We went from 'this could be rough' to 'we're completely dominating' in like ten minutes. Every time you touched something we got better gear."

He gestures enthusiastically.

"And now I have a living flying vehicle that's bonded to me specifically. Do you know how cool that is?" He's genuinely excited. "Plus the combat was solid, the coordination worked great, and we handled a dimensional incursion without breaking a sweat."

He looks at Sue preemptively.

"I'm being professional. But also: this was genuinely awesome and I absolutely want to do more missions like this."

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue takes a moment to formulate her response.

"Operationally: flawless." She's being thorough. "Communication was clear, tactical roles were well-defined, and coordination improved as the mission progressed. The force multiplication effect from your ability exceeded projections significantly."

She looks at the four new vehicles, then at Salvage's upgraded motor-home.

"Strategically: this changes our operational capacity. Each team member gained dimensional-hardened equipment and autonomous vehicles tailored to our specific roles. That's a permanent capability increase from a single mission."

She's professional but there's approval in her tone.

"Personally: you coordinate well, you're tactically sound, and you enhanced the team's effectiveness rather than just adding firepower." She pauses. "I'd recommend future collaboration to Fury without hesitation. This was a productive partnership."

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed has been waiting for this question.

He takes a breath.

"From a tactical perspective: exceptional. From a scientific perspective: this is the most fascinating phenomenon I've encountered in months and I have approximately four hundred questions about the underlying mechanics and I desperately want to examine the biotech vehicles and analyze the dimensional integration and study the equipment generation process and—"

Sue clears her throat.

Reed stops. Recalibrates.

"What I mean is: the teamup was extremely effective and I would very much like to collaborate again. Both for operational missions and, if you're willing, for scientific analysis of the capabilities we just witnessed."

He glances at his sensor-equipped biotech vehicle.

"I received a vehicle with dimensional sensory arrays that integrate perfectly with my analytical framework. That shouldn't be possible without weeks of calibration but it's _already operational_." He's struggling to stay calm. "The intelligence behind the generation process is extraordinary."

He looks at Salvage.

"So yes. Thoughts are: very positive. Please let's do this again. And also please let me study everything if you're comfortable with that level of scientific collaboration."

---

**BEN GRIMM - ADDITION**

Ben adds something.

"Reed's about ten seconds from askin' if he can take your vehicle apart piece by piece." He's amused. "Just warnin' you now."

Reed: "I wouldn't take it APART, I would just conduct non-invasive scans and—"

---

**JOHNNY STORM - PRACTICAL QUESTION**

Johnny has a follow-up.

"So if we do more missions together, we keep getting stronger?" He's thinking through the progression. "Like, every dimensional threat we fight, we get better dimensional gear?"

He's clearly interested in the escalation potential.

---

**SUE STORM - STRATEGIC OBSERVATION**

Sue adds a strategic note.

"The ability distributed rewards across the entire team based on contribution and role. That's unprecedented." She's thinking through implications. "Most powers are individual. Yours creates force multiplication for coordinated operations."

She looks at Salvage.

"That makes you extremely valuable for team missions. S.H.I.E.L.D. will want to leverage this capability. So will we, if you're interested in ongoing collaboration."

**REED RICHARDS - RESTRAINT BREAKING**

Reed is trying very hard to wait for the appropriate moment to ask technical questions.

The portal collapse has twelve minutes remaining.

He's not sure he can wait twelve minutes.

"The dimensional firming streams that flowed to your vehicle—" He can't help himself. "—were those direct matter-energy conversion or dimensional substrate integration and is the process reversible or does it represent permanent dimensional anchoring and—"

Sue: "Reed."

Reed: "I'll wait until after portal closure to ask detailed questions."

He does not sound convincing.

---

**BEN GRIMM - SUMMARY**

Ben looks at Salvage.

"Short version: we all think the teamup worked great and we'd do it again." He jerks his thumb at Reed. "Stretch here wants to study everything you got. Johnny's excited about his new toy. Sue's thinkin' strategically about future ops."

He pauses.

"And I got a vehicle that weighs about six tons and is covered in dimensional bug armor, which is not somethin' I thought I'd be sayin' today."

He's matter-of-fact about the absurdity.

"But yeah. Good mission. Good teamup. Let's do it again sometime."

The portal is now down to one meter diameter and shrinking steadily.

Reed's containment equipment is functioning perfectly.

The mission is entering final closure phase.

Johnny is eyeing his vehicle and clearly planning to test it the moment Sue gives permission.

"That's a significant long-term strategic advantage." She's being professional but there's clear approval. "Recurring team missions would create sustainable force multiplication for both parties."

He continues:

Ororo adds gently:

**COLOSSUS**

Piotr nods agreement.

---

Kitty is more immediate.

"I don't care if it's god-designed or magic or cosmic or whatever!" She's enthusiastic. "I care that it WORKS and it's COOL and training is gonna be FUN and we're gonna get BETTER and everyone WINS!"

She's summing up her priorities clearly.

"Everything else is just… details!"

---

**THE GRANDMASTER'S SANCTUM**

En Dwi Gast is watching Salvage describe the power as "god-designed."

"Technically accurate." He's amused. "Not a god by most definitions. But cosmic entity of immense power deliberately designing capability system? Close enough."

He watches the X-Men process the enjoyment factor.

"The Iso-8 integration DOES make training enjoyable. Not accidental. Designed feature. Practice with equipment strengthens integration, familiarizes user with capabilities, develops expertise." He's noting the feedback loop. "Making practice enjoyable ensures it happens frequently. Better outcomes. Faster progression."

He watches Xavier note the psychological optimization.

"Xavier's suspicious. Not overtly. But he's recognizing the design is TOO coherent for random manifestation. Too psychologically optimized." The Grandmaster is satisfied. "He suspects intentional design. He's correct. But he has no data on designer or mechanism."

He watches the team accept the enjoyment factor enthusiastically.

"They're not questioning origin. They're embracing outcome. Practical response. Especially from Shadowcat - 'details don't matter, results do.'"

---

**TOWN PERIMETER - ACCEPTANCE**

The X-Men are processing another layer of power optimization.

Not just tactical capability.

Not just psychological reinforcement.

But intrinsic enjoyment of practice.

Training becomes pleasure, not obligation.

Self-motivated skill development.

Sustainable high performance without burnout.

Another compounding benefit to coordination framework.

Shadowcat is already planning to train extensively.

Cyclops is planning to measure voluntary practice outcomes.

Storm is recognizing sustainable expertise development.

Colossus is satisfied with the coherent reward structure.

Xavier is noting the remarkable design optimization (with slight suspicion about "random manifestation" but no data to pursue that question).

Monthly coordination framework: even more appealing.

Mission complete. Return to Institute: imminent.

And everyone is looking forward to practicing with their new equipment.

Because it'll be fun.

**XAVIER INSTITUTE - VEHICLE BAY - THREE HOURS POST-MISSION**

**SHADOWCAT (KITTY PRYDE)**

Kitty is in the vehicle bay with her phasing stealth platform.

She's been "playing" for the last forty minutes without stopping.

Currently: phasing the entire vehicle halfway into the concrete floor, holding it there, then bringing it back up.

"Okay okay okay watch THIS—" She's calling to no one in particular.

She phases the vehicle, drives it THROUGH a support pillar, emerges on the other side.

"IT DOESN'T EVEN SLOW DOWN!" She's delighted. "The whole vehicle phases WITH me and there's NO drag! It's like driving through AIR except I'm driving through CONCRETE!"

---

**NEARBY - FORGE**

The mutant inventor is watching from a workbench, technical scanner in hand.

"The phase field extends exactly six inches beyond the vehicle surface perimeter." He's reading data. "Perfect consistency. No fluctuation. And it's drawing power from…" He frowns at the scanner. "I don't understand the power source. It reads as Sentinel-derived but the architecture is completely foreign."

---

**SHADOWCAT**

Kitty phases the vehicle up through the ceiling, into the room above, then back down.

"FORGE! Can you come up to the Rec Room?! I wanna see if I can phase through the floor while someone's standing on it!"

She's absolutely in exploration mode - testing boundaries, discovering capabilities, completely engaged.

---

**EXTERIOR GROUNDS - STORM'S ATMOSPHERIC PLATFORM**

Ororo is hovering two hundred feet above the Institute grounds.

Her atmospheric platform is sleek, aerial, responding to her control with perfect precision.

She's running systematic tests - but clearly ENJOYING them.

"Atmospheric pressure differential: point-three millibars." She's reading the enhanced sensors aloud. "Precipitation probability in current air mass: fourteen percent. Wind shear at current altitude: negligible."

She adjusts controls.

Lightning forms - not from her powers alone, but channeled through the platform's amplification systems.

The bolt strikes EXACTLY where she intended. Precision beyond her normal capability.

"Oh." She says it quietly. "Oh, this is remarkable."

---

He grins.

"She looks like a kid with a new chemistry set!"

---

**STORM**

Ororo doesn't respond - too focused on the platform.

She's generating micro-weather patterns. Small rain clouds. Localized wind currents. Precision atmospheric manipulation at scales she couldn't reliably manage before.

The platform isn't REPLACING her powers. It's making them SURGICAL.

"I could prevent lightning strikes in populated areas with this precision." She's thinking aloud. "Redirect storm systems around civilian zones. Create rain exactly where needed for drought relief without flooding."

She's discovering humanitarian applications through play.

Piotr is standing next to his heavy assault/rescue platform.

Just… holding the rescue equipment. Testing the grips. Moving controls through their range of motion.

---

Punch. Block. Pivot. Lifting motion.

"No restriction. No weight. Feels like…" He pauses, searching for words. "Feels like my own body. Not armor ON body. Just… stronger body."

He picks up a rescue tool from the platform. Heavy lifting jack designed for his strength level.

The grip is perfectly sized for his large hands.

He operates it. Smooth. Effortless. Natural.

"This is first time equipment feels RIGHT." He's being very honest. "Always before: too small, awkward angle, uncomfortable grip. This is…"

He doesn't finish. Just keeps operating the tool, experiencing proper ergonomics for the first time in his enhanced life.

**HERBIE's voice through speakers:** "Affirmative, Cyclops. The command suite's processing architecture is extraordinarily sophisticated. Comparable to S.H.I.E.L.D. Helicarrier command systems but with more intuitive interface design."

Scott brings up a tactical scenario - hypothetical Sentinel attack on the Institute.

The command platform immediately generates optimal defensive positioning, evacuation routes, engagement priorities.

"It's doing threat assessment automatically." Scott sounds impressed despite his controlled tone. "I input scenario, it provides strategic options. Not orders. OPTIONS. With probability assessments for each approach."

He's clearly finding this engaging.

She's noting the body language.

"He's enjoying it."

---

**CYCLOPS**

Scott runs another scenario. Then another.

He's experiencing well-designed UI for the first time.

Discovery: All equipment is interoperable

Implication: Unified capability network

Concern: Dependency on voluntary cooperation

Risk: Asset has enormous political leverage

The system works beautifully until it doesn't.

And they can't prevent it from stopping.

Meanwhile Salvage is thrilled because he gets to see the world and meet new and interesting people and collect exotic equipment and be a superhero. He has no intention of weaponizing any of this. He thinks working with more and more people is awesome. The only thing he’s unhappy about is stuff like North Korea (just a single example). He wishes that everyone would be kind but is very afraid of what would happen if he tried to leverage his situation to make the world better. “Russia, be nicer to gay people or else. Oh shit, there’s a red laser dot on my chest now” is something he’s thinking about as an example. He’s hoping that international cooperation will encourage folks to consider different perspectives. He is genuinely worried about things and this is part of why he pushes for oversight. He thinks that if there’s enough oversight and folks influencing him he “won’t fuck up”.

These thoughts slowly leak out. He doesn’t say anything explicitly but he just gives stray thoughts every now and then that build up a picture of what he’s thinking about all of this.

"Council is worried he'll weaponize leverage. Asset is worried he'll accidentally cause international incident by refusing coordination with wrong nation at wrong time."

"He wants world peace but knows he'd get killed for trying to leverage his position to achieve it."

---

**MARIA HILL**

**MARIA HILL**

"Recommendation: Asset anxiety about responsibility suggests low risk of deliberate leverage exploitation. But HIGH risk of paralysis or withdrawal if he feels pressure is too great."

**FURY**

---

**MARIA HILL**

**FURY**

He pauses.

Consistent pattern: enthusiasm about coordination, anxiety about responsibility, idealistic preferences tempered by sophisticated understanding of consequences."

"Asset wants global cooperation. Recognizes he cannot enforce it through leverage without creating problems. Explicitly stated concern about 'accidentally causing international incidents' and 'making things worse.'"

**COUNCIL MEMBER 4 (MALE, AMERICAN)**

**COUNCIL MEMBER 5 (MALE, RUSSIAN ACCENT)**

---

**COUNCIL CHAIR**

**COUNCIL MEMBER 4**

---

**FURY**

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 5**

---

**COUNCIL CHAIR**

**COUNCIL MEMBER 2**

**COUNCIL MEMBER 3**

**COUNCIL MEMBER 4**

The system adapts.

Asset gets protective oversight he explicitly wants.

Institutions get diplomatic control they explicitly need.

Everyone's happy.

Because asset spent years learning what NOT to do.

And is doing the opposite.

He's leaning forward, focused.

He watches Salvage's nervous response in the strategy session.

"Protagonist is scared. Good. Should be scared. This isn't street-level anymore. This is COSMIC-level. Win or lose, this fight determines everything."

He watches the countdown.

The game is escalating.

Hero coordination: Maximum scale

Diplomatic approach: Prepared

Combat readiness: Developing

Salvage: Nervous but committed

Against a being that judges planets.

It's dimensional-capable mobile command platform with cosmic-grade systems.

---

**SALVAGE'S PERSONAL ARMOR**

The suit that started with AIM exosuit and Kree exo-frame.

Layered across 45+ encounters.

Now: CELESTIAL INTEGRATION.

Not god-level. But substantially elevated.

**Structural integrity:** Can withstand cosmic-level impacts. Dimensional stability. Reality-anchoring properties.

**Power systems:** Celestial energy matrix. Effectively unlimited operational duration.

**Neural interface:** Enhanced to process cosmic-level threat data. Reaction time improvement beyond previous human-standard augmentation.

**Defensive systems:** Hard-light shield reinforced with reality-stabilization. Mystical wards operating on cosmic permanence. Multi-spectrum protection including dimensional, temporal, and reality-warping threats.

**Offensive capabilities:** Repulsor systems enhanced to Celestial-energy projection. Magnetic pulse expanded to reality-manipulation field. Can affect cosmic-grade materials.

**Mobility:** Can operate in dimensional barriers. Limited flight in cosmic void. Reality-anchoring prevents dimensional displacement.

**Threat recognition:** Now identifies cosmic-level entities, dimensional incursions, reality anomalies. Ultron recognition expanded to Celestial-class AI detection.

The armor that made him superhuman now makes him capable of engaging cosmic-level threats.

Not as equal. But as PARTICIPANT.

---

**IMMEDIATE RESPONSES - GLOBAL**

**AVENGERS TOWER - BLACK WIDOW**

Natasha is examining the tactical systems that manifested on her person.

"These sensors can detect biological signatures across dimensional barriers." She's reading the interface. "I can track targets through reality distortions."

She looks at the assembled team.

"Everyone just became significantly more capable. All of us. Simultaneously."

---

**BAXTER BUILDING - REED RICHARDS**

Reed is analyzing the equipment with barely contained scientific excitement.

"Celestial-derived technology. The analytical capabilities are…" He's running diagnostics. "I'm detecting quantum states I couldn't observe before. Dimensional frequencies beyond previous instrument range."

He looks at his team.

"This is civilization-level capability leap. Not just for us. For EVERYONE who participated."

---

**XAVIER INSTITUTE - PROFESSOR XAVIER**

Charles is examining the Cerebro enhancement systems.

"The psychic amplification range has increased by orders of magnitude. I can now detect mental signatures across continental distances with precision I've never achieved."

He's thoughtful.

"Every X-Men member received enhancement. Our students who provided support received protective equipment."

He pauses.

"This changes our operational capacity fundamentally."

---

**S.H.I.E.L.D. HELICARRIER - MARIA HILL**

Hill is looking at the Celestial-derived command systems.

And at the hundreds of S.H.I.E.L.D. personnel examining their new equipment.

"Sir, we just equipped our entire operational force with Celestial technology." She's reporting to Fury. "Combat teams. Technical staff. Medical personnel. Command structure. Even civilian analysts."

She pauses.

"This is institutional transformation, not individual enhancement."

---

**CANADIAN DEPARTMENT H - DIRECTOR DUGAN**

Dugan is receiving reports from Alpha Flight and support personnel.

"Guardian's vehicle has full dimensional navigation. The team received comprehensive enhancement. Our coordination staff received command equipment."

He's looking at the scope.

"Canada just became substantially more capable as sovereign defense force."

---

**THE GRANDMASTER'S SANCTUM**

En Dwi Gast is watching the global equipment distribution.

"Celestial energy drain. Hundreds of participants. Every organization. Every contribution level."

He watches Salvage's vehicle systems complete.

"Dimensional navigation: operational. Celestial power core: active. Cosmic-grade systems: complete."

He watches Salvage's armor enhancement.

"Not god-tier. But cosmic-capable. Can engage Celestial-class threats as participant rather than observer."

He leans back.

"The game just escalated permanently. Protagonist has cosmic-capable mobile platform and armor. Global hero community has Celestial-derived equipment. Everyone who helped coordinate has protective systems."

He's satisfied with the combat.

Now watching for consequences.

"World response incoming. This won't be universally positive."

---

**GLOBAL NEWS - 6 HOURS POST-CELESTIAL**

**CNN - BREAKING NEWS**

"Reports confirm: hundreds of individuals across multiple nations received advanced technology during Celestial engagement. Equipment appeared spontaneously, distributed to combat forces and support personnel alike."

"The source: Enhanced individual known as Salvage, whose power generates equipment from defeated entities. The Celestial was not killed, raising questions about how equipment generation occurred."

---

**BBC WORLD SERVICE**

"Unprecedented capability distribution across international forces. British Enhanced Response received technology beyond current national development capacity. Questions emerge: who controls this equipment? What oversight exists? What happens to global power balance?"

---

**RUSSIAN STATE NEWS**

"Russian Enhanced Bureau personnel equipped with advanced systems during Celestial response. Federal Security Service confirms equipment integration successful. Analysts question: dependency on American-affiliated asset for capability enhancement creates strategic vulnerability."

---

**SOCIAL MEDIA - IMMEDIATE RESPONSE**

**Twitter/X trends:** #CelestialTech - 4.2M tweets #Salvage - 3.8M tweets  
#GlobalDefense - 2.1M tweets

**Responses mixed:**

"They saved the world AND equipped global defense forces. This is incredible."

"One person just distributed military technology to hundreds of people across multiple nations without democratic oversight. That's terrifying."

"So if you were RICH enough to have heroes on payroll during Celestial fight, you got advanced tech. If you're regular person, you got nothing. Cool. Cool cool cool."

"Canada got Celestial tech. UK got Celestial tech. France, Japan, Germany, Russia - all got equipment. Who DIDN'T get invited to this party?"

"S.H.I.E.L.D. civilian analyst got advanced technology for doing threat calculations. But actual soldiers fighting conventional threats got nothing. Explain the fairness."

---

**WORLD SECURITY COUNCIL - EMERGENCY SESSION - 12 HOURS POST-CELESTIAL**

**COUNCIL CHAIR**

"Celestial threat resolved. Equipment distribution confirmed across hundreds of participants. Strategic implications require immediate assessment."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 2**

"Every participating nation received Celestial-derived technology. But nations that DIDN'T participate received nothing." Her voice is measured. "This creates capability gap between participants and non-participants that's unprecedented."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 3**

"China did not participate. India did not participate. Multiple South American, African, and Middle Eastern nations did not participate." He's listing. "They're now substantially behind in capability development compared to participating nations."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 5**

"Russia participated minimally. Single operation prior to Celestial engagement. Our equipment distribution was limited compared to nations with sustained coordination history." His tone carries concern. "United States, Canada, United Kingdom with months of coordination received comprehensive enhancement. Russia received baseline systems."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 4**

"The distribution wasn't equal. It was PROPORTIONAL. Nations and organizations with sustained Salvage coordination received more comprehensive equipment than nations with minimal coordination."

He pauses.

"That creates incentive structure: more coordination equals more capability. But it also creates resentment from excluded nations."

---

**FURY - PRESENT**

"Equipment distribution followed participation pattern. Everyone who contributed received appropriate enhancement. Combat forces received combat systems. Support personnel received support equipment. Civilian coordinators received analytical tools."

He's precise.

"The mechanism doesn't discriminate. It rewards CONTRIBUTION."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 2**

"Which nations interpret as discriminatory. China is calling this 'technological imperialism through selective distribution.' India is demanding access to Salvage coordination framework. Multiple nations are protesting exclusion from capability enhancement."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 3**

"And they're not wrong. Celestial technology distributed to Western allies plus Russia and Japan. Excluded: most of Asia, Africa, South America, Middle East."

He's being direct.

"This looks like deliberate capability hoarding by established powers."

---

**COUNCIL CHAIR**

"Director Fury, coordination framework parameters. Who determines which nations participate?"

---

**FURY**

"Institutional pre-approval process established three months ago. Council reviews coordination requests. Approves based on legitimate threat response, institutional oversight, and diplomatic considerations."

He pauses.

"China hasn't requested coordination. Neither has India. They're protesting exclusion from framework they haven't attempted to join."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 5**

"Because framework requires transparency and oversight they're unwilling to provide. China won't submit enhanced programs to international review. India won't coordinate enhanced forces under Council authority."

He continues:

"They want benefits without requirements. Now they're angry that nations meeting requirements received benefits they didn't."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 2**

"Politically accurate. Strategically irrelevant. Perception is: Western powers equipped themselves with Celestial technology while excluding developing nations."

She pulls up protest documentation.

"UN General Assembly emergency session scheduled. Multiple nations demanding access to Salvage coordination. Others demanding technology sharing from equipped nations. Some calling for international oversight of Salvage's power itself."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 4**

"We can't share Celestial-derived technology without Salvage's power to integrate it. Equipment is personalized to recipients. Transferring it doesn't work."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 3**

"Which makes Salvage coordination access even more valuable. And makes excluded nations even more resentful."

---

**COUNCIL CHAIR**

"Recommendations?"

Long silence.

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 2**

"Expand framework. Offer coordination access to additional nations willing to meet oversight requirements."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 5**

"That dilutes capability enhancement for existing participants. More teams means less equipment per team from future operations."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 3**

"Unless we increase operation frequency. More coordinations means more total equipment generation despite broader distribution."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 4**

"Which requires Salvage's cooperation. He's already coordinating two to three operations weekly. Increasing frequency means increased pressure on voluntary cooperation we cannot compel."

---

**COUNCIL CHAIR**

"We return to fundamental dependency: voluntary cooperation from single individual for access to capability enhancement framework."

Pause.

"Except now the SCALE of that dependency is visible globally. Hundreds equipped. Multiple nations enhanced. Excluded nations demanding access."

Another pause.

"And Salvage is now cosmic-capable with dimensional navigation platform and enhanced armor."

---

**COUNCIL MEMBER 2**

"Asset who started with motorcycle and basic armor now has dimensional-capable mobile fortress and cosmic-grade personal systems."

She's being precise.

"If he withdraws cooperation, equipped forces maintain existing gear but no additional development occurs. That's hundreds of Celestial-equipped individuals frozen at current capability while threats continue evolving."

---

**COUNCIL CHAIR**

"Strategic assessment: Celestial engagement demonstrated cooperation framework effectiveness. Also demonstrated dependency vulnerability and exclusion consequences."

Pause.

"Session adjourned. Individual member consultations with national governments regarding framework expansion and diplomatic management of excluded nations."

---

**PRIVATE CHANNELS - MULTIPLE CONVERSATIONS**

**UN Headquarters - China's Representative:**

"United States coordinates enhanced individuals with cosmic-capable asset. Distributes advanced technology to allied nations. Excludes China from framework."

"This is strategic capability control disguised as international cooperation. We demand access or we develop alternative frameworks."

**India's Minister of Defense:**

"Western coordination excludes developing nations while claiming global cooperation. India has enhanced programs. India has threat response capability. We're excluded because we won't submit to Western oversight structures."

"If framework won't include us, we'll establish our own enhanced response networks."

**African Union Security Council:**

"Entire continent excluded from Celestial technology distribution. Western nations equipped. Asian allies equipped. Africa ignored."

"This perpetuates technological dependency and capability gaps that keep developing nations reliant on Western defense."

**South American Coalition:**

"Enhanced individuals exist in South America. Threats exist in South America. But coordination framework operates through North American and European structures only."

"We're second-class participants in our own defense."

---

**S.H.I.E.L.D. HELICARRIER - FURY AND HILL**

**MARIA HILL**

"Global response is fragmenting. Participating nations celebrating. Excluded nations protesting. UN demanding framework expansion. China and India talking about alternative coordination structures."

---

**FURY**

"Predictable. Celestial technology distribution made capability gap visible. Nations that coordinated became substantially stronger. Nations that didn't are now substantially behind."

He's reviewing diplomatic channels.

"And they're not asking to join framework with oversight requirements. They're demanding access without requirements or threatening to create competing frameworks."

---

**MARIA HILL**

"Can we expand framework to include them?"

---

**FURY**

"Technically yes. Salvage accepts coordination with legitimate hero teams. China has enhanced programs. India has capability. We could coordinate."

He pauses.

"But they won't accept institutional oversight. Won't provide transparency. Won't submit enhanced programs to Council review. They want equipment without requirements."

---

**MARIA HILL**

"And if we accommodate that, existing participants will protest preferential treatment for nations that don't meet framework standards."

---

**FURY**

"Yes. We built cooperation framework on institutional oversight. Removing oversight for some nations undermines framework for all nations."

He closes the diplomatic reports.

"Celestial was defeated. Global defense is enhanced. And now we manage political consequences of success."

---

**THE GRANDMASTER'S SANCTUM**

En Dwi Gast is watching the global reactions.

Not with interest in politics.

With interest in PRESSURE on the protagonist.

"Hundreds equipped. Multiple nations enhanced. Excluded nations demanding access. Political complexity escalating."

He watches the UN emergency session scheduling.

"Protagonist just became center of international political dispute. Nations demanding coordination. Others protesting expansion. Some threatening alternative frameworks."

He pulls up Salvage's psychological profile.

"Asset who's anxious about responsibility just equipped hundreds of people with Celestial technology. Asset who's afraid of 'fucking up' is now facing international political pressure."

He's watching for breaking points.

Not because he cares about politics.

Because protagonist under pressure makes better game.

"Week forty-eight. Cosmic capability achieved. Global equipment distribution complete. International political consequences emerging."

He leans forward.

"How does anxious self-educated protagonist handle being center of international capability dispute?"

That's the interesting question.

Not the politics.

The PROTAGONIST'S response to the politics.

The game continues.

Salvage returns to his mobile fortress. He looks at his new systems. Decides to report them to his WSC contacts by sending a file. Keep in mind his Dual AI cores: navigation/operation + threat analysis/strategic assessment (consulting each other) and how they’ve been updated to be sentient, same as his suit ai.

Remember the rules. Keep in mind the information asymmetries. Salvage + AI shouldn’t know about the political clusterfuck yet. Don’t do his lines.
